An LLM-assisted voice-control module converts spoken instructions into high-level locomotion commands, while the manipulation module retargets human hand motions to the robot through inverse kinematics and PD control. The system also records multimodal data, including egocentric RGB observations, voice/text commands, joint states, hand motions, and eye-gaze signals, supporting future imitation learning and autonomy. We evaluate the framework on a Unitree H1 humanoid equipped with dexterous hands in manipulation and social interaction tasks. Results show that novice users can successfully operate the system after brief familiarization, achieving 80\% success in object manipulation and 70\% success in a social cube-passing task. These results demonstrate the potential of immersive, language-assisted teleoperation as an accessible interface for humanoid interaction, remote assistance, and multimodal data collection.

</details>


### [144] [GeoGS-SLAM: Geometry-Only Gaussian Splatting for Dense Monocular SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07452)
*Lipu Zhou,Yaoyun Kang,Junxiang Pang,Shengkai Sun,Tingting Bao,Kehan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅关注几何重建的高斯点阵表示方法GeoGS及其对应的稠密视觉SLAM系统GeoGS-SLAM，通过去除外观建模显著提升了效率和几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS方法同时建模外观与几何，但在SLAM任务中几何更为关键，因此探索是否可在无外观建模下进行3D重建具有重要意义。

Method: 提出Geometry-only Gaussian Splatting (GeoGS)，仅保留空间参数，并设计单视图与多视图下的几何与光度监督训练框架，结合局部平面驱动初始化和地图更新策略用于闭环。

Result: 相比现有方法减少了80%以上的每基元参数量，加速了几何收敛，提升光照鲁棒性，在合成与真实场景基准上均优于SOTA方法。

Conclusion: GeoGS能够有效实现无需外观建模的高质量几何重建，为面向机器人任务的稠密SLAM提供了更高效、稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Dense visual SLAM is a fundamental problem in robotics. Recent advances in 3DGS have demonstrated its potential for dense SLAM. Existing 3DGS frameworks focus on both appearance and geometry modeling. However, scene geometry is typically more critical for SLAM than novel view synthesis because downstream robotic tasks, such as navigation and obstacle avoidance, rely primarily on accurate spatial geometry rather than photorealistic rendering. This observation raises a natural question: Is it feasible for 3DGS to perform 3D reconstruction without scene appearance modeling? Motivated by this, we propose Geometry-only Gaussian Splatting (GeoGS), which directly reconstructs scene geometry, and further present GeoGS-SLAM, a dense visual SLAM system built upon this representation. Specifically, GeoGS retains only spatial parameters to reduce the number of per-primitive parameters by over 80%. In contrast to existing 3DGS methods, GeoGS focuses solely on geometric reconstruction, which significantly reduces the number of Gaussian primitives, accelerates geometric convergence, and enhances robustness to illumination variations. In addition, we present an effective training framework that optimizes the Gaussian primitives via single-view and multi-view geometric and photometric supervision, and speeds up geometry convergence with a local-plane driven initialization that better aligns primitives with local structures. Furthermore, we introduce a map update strategy for loop closure that globally transforms the Gaussian map to align it with the corrected pose estimates, thereby preventing map tearing caused by inconsistent per-viewpoint pose corrections in existing methods. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms SOTA methods in terms of online mapping efficiency and geometric reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [145] [EmbodiedGen V2: An Agentic, Simulation-Ready 3D World Engine for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07459)
*Xinjie Wang,Liu Liu,Taojun Ding,Andrew Choi,Chaodong Huang,Mengao Zhao,Ziang Li,Jackson Jiang,Chunlei Yu,Shengxiang Liu,Wei Xu,Zhizhong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmbodiedGen V2是一个生成式3D世界引擎，用于构建可执行的、适用于具身智能的仿真环境，支持资产自动生成与组装、多任务仿真和策略训练，并实现从仿真到真实机器人的高效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D资产生成技术发展迅速，但将这些资产组合成可用于策略训练的仿真环境仍依赖大量人工操作，限制了闭环学习的可扩展性。因此需要一个自动化、可复用的生成框架来填补这一空白。

Method: 提出EmbodiedGen V2，采用统一的仿真就绪表示方法，整合跨仿真器资产、交互能力、任务驱动世界、大规模多房间场景和有状态的Vibe Coding，构建一个可生成、可编辑、可重用的仿真流程。

Result: 资产管线达到96.5%的人工接受率和98.6%的碰撞检测成功率；83.3%的任务驱动世界无需手动修改即可用于下游仿真；在线强化学习使仿真成功率从9.7%提升至79.8%，并成功迁移到真实机器人，任务成功率从21.7%提升至75.0%。

Conclusion: EmbodiedGen V2为具身策略的训练、评估与部署提供了可扩展的仿真基础设施，显著提升了仿真到现实的迁移效率和自动化水平。

Abstract: We present EmbodiedGen V2, a generative 3D world engine for building executable sim-ready environments for embodied intelligence. Sim-ready 3D asset generation has advanced rapidly, yet assembling such assets into policy-ready task environments remains largely manual, limiting scalable closed-loop learning. EmbodiedGen V2 addresses this gap through a unified sim-ready representation that connects cross-simulator assets, interaction affordances, task-driven worlds, large-scale multi-room scenes, and stateful Vibe Coding into a generative, editable, and reusable simulation pipeline. The generated environments support manipulation, navigation, mobile manipulation, cross-simulator deployment, and embodied policy training. In evaluation, the asset pipeline achieves 96.5% human acceptance and 98.6% collision success, and 83.3% of task-driven worlds are directly usable for downstream simulation without manual modification. Online reinforcement learning with generated environments further improves simulation success from 9.7% to 79.8%, and transfers to real robots with task success increasing from 21.7% to 75.0%. These results establish EmbodiedGen V2 as scalable simulation infrastructure for training, evaluating, and deploying embodied policies.

</details>


### [146] [Agent-Exploitation Affordances: From Basic to Complex Representation Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07475)
*Bastien Dussard,Aurélie Clodic,Guillaume Sarthou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可操作的本体表示方法来描述社会情境下的合作性可供性，使人工代理能够在多智能体环境中扩展其行为可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人学中，现有研究较少关注社会情境下多智能体之间的交互如何扩展各自的行为可能性，尤其是在合作性可供性方面的表示问题尚未解决。

Method: 提出一种可处理的合作性可供性本体表示方法，并通过组合基本模式来构建多样化的应用场景以验证其有效性。

Result: 该本体能够有效表示多智能体间的合作行为，并成功应用于多种复杂场景的建模。

Conclusion: 合作性可供性的本体表示有助于提升人工代理在社会情境中的交互与协作能力，为社交机器人提供了理论支持和实现路径。

Abstract: In robotics, the capability of an artificial agent to represent the range of its action possibilities, i.e. affordances, is crucial to understand how it can act on its environment. While functional affordances, which refer to the use of tools and objects, have been broadly studied in knowledge representation, the implications of a social context and the presence of other agents have remained unexplored in this field. Consequently, in the field of social robotics, a multi-agent context enables the agents to engage in new actions that are potentially complementary to their individual capabilities, leading to the perspective of agentexploitation. This work focuses on the concept of cooperative affordance within the realm of social affordances. Cooperative affordances refer to situations where agents interact with each other to extend their action possibilities range. From this definition, this paper proposes a tractable ontological representation of this concept with the aim of making it usable by an artificial agent. Expanding on those elementary patterns, we illustrate the effectiveness of these representations by combining them to depict a diverse range of scenarios.

</details>


### [147] [Smooth Operator: A Real-Time Sampling-Based Algorithm for Kinematic Hand Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07491)
*Robert Jomar Malate,Erik Bauer,Norica Bacuieti,Stefanos Charalambous,Elvis Nava,Robert K. Katzschmann,Benedek Forrai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的基于采样的无梯度重定向方法SBR，用于低抖动、实时的运动学重定向，在仿真和真实用户实验中均表现出更高的任务成功率和更低的操作疲劳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于梯度的重定向算法容易陷入局部最优，导致抖动问题，影响遥操作数据质量和用户体验。

Method: 提出了Sampling-Based Retargeter (SBR)，一种受采样控制文献启发的无梯度运动学重定向方法，专为低抖动和实时性设计。

Result: 在仿真和18名参与者的用户研究中，SBR相比梯度法基线取得了最高的任务成功率（54.1%），并显著降低认知疲劳，NASA-TLX工作负荷评分最低（36.4/100）。

Conclusion: SBR是一种高效且直观的灵巧操作重定向方法，并为未来重定向研究提供了严谨的基准测试方法。

Abstract: Advances in learning-based robotic manipulation, such as Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models and Video Action Models (VAMs), heavily rely on high-quality teleoperation data. Their capabilities are strictly upper-bounded by the quality of the underlying human demonstrations. Current gradient-based retargeting algorithms often converge to different local minima, resulting in jitter that affects data quality and teleoperation experience. To address this, we introduce the Sampling-Based Retargeter (SBR), a novel gradient-free retargeting method drawn from the rich literature of sampling-based control and explicitly designed for low-jitter, real-time kinematic retargeting. We evaluate SBR both in simulation and through a rigorous real-world user study involving 18 participants performing 3 complex manipulation tasks. Compared to gradient-based baselines, SBR achieved the highest overall task success rate (54.1%) while significantly reducing operator cognitive fatigue, recording the lowest NASA-TLX workload score (36.4 out of 100). Ultimately, we establish SBR as a highly effective, intuitive retargeter for dexterous manipulation, providing the community with a rigorous benchmarking methodology to guide future retargeting research.

</details>


### [148] [Generating Personalized Lower-Limb Kinematics Across Walking Speeds Using Subject-Conditioned Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07533)
*Diya Dinesh,Adrian Krieger,Changseob Song,Dongho Park,Aaron J. Young,Inseung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于残差扩散模型的框架，可根据个体在单一行走速度下的步态数据生成未见过速度下的个性化下肢运动学，显著减少外骨骼个性化所需的大量数据采集负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化外骨骼辅助需要大量个体在不同行走任务中的步态数据，但传统动作捕捉过程成本高、耗时长，尤其对临床人群负担重，特别是在不同行走速度间步态变化显著的情况下。

Method: 提出一种以主体为条件的残差扩散模型，利用在可见速度下的矢状面髋、膝、踝轨迹和目标不可见速度，通过基于Transformer的去噪网络生成残差，结合特征级线性调制实现轨迹转换。模型仅在健康人数据上训练。

Result: 在未参与训练的健康受试者上达到3.4°的平均绝对误差（MAE），在未经微调的中风患者上仍保持6.0° MAE，且保留了临床步态的个体特征；相比监督前馈基线误差降低70%以上，单个速度输入即可达到四个速度输入的精度水平（误差差<0.4°）。

Conclusion: 主体条件化的残差扩散模型能仅用最少的输入数据，准确合成跨速度的个性化步态，有效减轻下游外骨骼个性化所需的数据采集负担，具有良好的泛化性和临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Personalizing exoskeleton assistance requires user-specific gait data across many locomotor tasks, yet collecting this data demands repeated motion capture sessions that are costly, time-intensive, and especially burdensome for clinical populations. This challenge is most acute across walking speeds, where gait changes substantially and deviates further in clinical gait. This work introduces a subject-conditioned residual diffusion framework that generates personalized lower-limb kinematics at unseen walking speeds from a subject's gait sequence at a single seen speed. Given sagittal-plane hip, knee, and ankle trajectories at a seen speed and a desired unseen speed, the model generates a residual that transforms the seen trajectory into the unseen one, using a transformer denoiser conditioned on the subject's gait and the two speeds through feature-wise linear modulation. Trained only on able-bodied data, the model achieved a mean absolute error (MAE) of 3.4° on held-out able-bodied subjects. Without any stroke-specific fine-tuning, it achieved a 6.0° MAE on out-of-training-distribution stroke subjects, retaining subject identity for clinical gait. The framework reduced the MAE by over 70% relative to supervised feed-forward baselines, and a single seen speed matched the accuracy of four speeds within 0.4°. These results demonstrate that subject-conditioned residual diffusion can synthesize personalized gait across speeds from minimal data, reducing the collection burden for downstream exoskeleton personalization.

</details>


### [149] [SonoRank: Towards Calibration-Free Real-Time Finger Flexion Detection from Forearm Ultrasound Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07542)
*Dean Zadok,Alon Wolf,Alex M. Bronstein,Oren Salzman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SonoRank方法，通过学习超声序列的相对运动幅度排序，实现无需校准的手指屈曲检测，显著提升F1分数，推动超声波假肢向实用化发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于超声的假肢控制方法需要针对每个用户进行校准，限制了其商业化应用，因此需要一种无需校准的通用控制方法。

Method: SonoRank首先学习对前臂超声视频序列按各手指相对运动幅度进行排序，利用学习到的表征，在操作开始时捕获的静息参考基础上微调以分类各手指是否主动屈曲。

Result: 在12名受试者的同步运动数据集上进行12折留一交叉验证，SonoRank相比直接分类基线方法F1分数提升了28%。

Conclusion: 成对排序是一种有效的预训练信号，可用于受试者无关的假肢控制，为超声波驱动假肢的无校准实际部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Powered prosthetic hands are frequently abandoned, largely due to the limited functionality of current devices that rely on surface electromyography (sEMG). Sonomyography (ultrasound) has emerged as a promising alternative, owing to its ability to observe muscle activity in real time and control a greater number of degrees of freedom. Yet, existing ultrasound-based methods require per-user fine-tuning, limiting their commercialization. We propose SonoRank, an important step towards calibration-free finger flexion detection from forearm ultrasound video. SonoRank first learns to rank pairs of ultrasound sequences by their relative motion magnitude for each of the five fingers. The learned representations are then fine-tuned to classify whether each finger is actively flexing, using a rest reference that is captured at the beginning of the operation. Under 12-fold leave-one-subject-out cross-validation on a dataset of twelve subjects with synchronized kinematics, SonoRank achieves a 28% improvement in F1 score over direct classification baselines that skip the ranking stage. These results establish pairwise ranking as an effective pretraining signal for subject-independent control, bringing ultrasound-based prosthetics closer to practical, calibration-free deployment.

</details>


### [150] [Context-Aware Force Estimation for Deformable Tool Manipulation in Robotic Environmental Swabbing via Few-Shot Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07574)
*Siavash Mahmoudi,Chaitainya Kuppar Reddy,Yang Tian,Dongyi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数据驱动的可变形工具操作（DTM）接触力估计框架，利用递归神经网络（LSTM）结合少样本自适应策略，在无需精确物理模型或末端集成传感器的情况下，实现了对未知表面和工具刚度变化的鲁棒力估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于柔性工具的动力学特性引入了非线性粘弹性滞后效应，使得腕部力传感器难以准确反映末端真实接触力，且在无菌、一次性使用要求下无法部署内置传感器，因此需要一种不依赖物理模型和永久传感硬件的高精度、可泛化的接触力估计方法。

Method: 采用基于本体感知数据的序列建模方法，通过比较多种时序模型选择出性能最优的紧凑型LSTM作为骨干网络；为进一步提升在未见环境下的泛化能力，提出一种参数隔离的少样本自适应策略，利用FiLM模块将低维上下文嵌入调节至冻结的主干网络中，实现快速适应而不引发灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在UR5e平台上测试九种不同工具-表面交互场景表明，该方法在零样本条件下最多可将估计误差降低63%，同时保持原始性能，具备良好的跨域鲁棒性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过分离共享的形变历史动态与特定域的条件调节，所提方法能够在非平稳环境中实现可靠、高效的接触力估计，为可变形工具操作中的力感知提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic surface swabbing requires sustained interaction between a compliant tool and heterogeneous environments, where accurate estimation of tip-level contact force is critical for consistent sampling performance. However, deformable tool dynamics introduce nonlinear viscoelastic hysteresis that decouples wrist-mounted force measurements from true contact forces, while tool-integrated sensors are impractical for deployment due to sterility and disposability constraints. This paper presents a data-driven framework for contact force estimation in Deformable Tool Manipulation (DTM) that leverages proprioceptive sensing without requiring explicit physical models or permanent embedded sensing hardware at the tool tip. A recurrent architecture is first identified through a comparative evaluation of temporal models, where a compact LSTM achieves the lowest estimation error and sub-millisecond inference latency. To address generalization across unseen surfaces and tool compliance conditions, we introduce a parameter-isolated few-shot adaptation strategy that augments a frozen recurrent backbone with low-dimensional context embeddings using feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM). Experiments on a UR5e platform across nine tool-surface interaction regimes demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly improves robustness under domain shift, reducing zero-shot estimation error by up to 63\% while preserving baseline performance without catastrophic forgetting. These results show that separating shared deformation-history dynamics from domain-specific conditioning enables reliable force estimation for DTM in non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [151] [CARLA-GS: Decoupling Representation, Reasoning, and Physics Simulation for Autonomous Driving Corner-Case Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07601)
*Kaicong Huang,Meng Ma,Ruimin Ke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CARLA-GS的模块化框架，用于在自动驾驶仿真中生成具有光真实感的高危边缘案例。该框架结合了视觉表示、语义推理与物理执行，实现了语义意图与物理可行性的统一，并通过实验验证了其在时空一致性和可控性方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶的安全性评估依赖于罕见但关键的交互场景，现有方法在生成具有物理真实性和时空一致性的边缘案例方面存在不足，因此需要一个统一且模块化的生成框架。

Method: 提出CARLA-GS框架：从真实驾驶数据重建可编辑的高斯场景；利用多智能体大语言模型进行场景级语义推理并生成意图级轨迹；底层运动控制交由CARLA中的PID控制器完成；最后将车辆状态重投影至高斯场景实现以自我为中心的渲染。

Result: 在Waymo开放数据集上的实验表明，该方法能定量和定性地生成可控、光真实感强、时空一致且符合语义意图与物理可行性的边缘案例视频。

Conclusion: CARLA-GS通过解耦视觉表示、语义推理与物理执行，同时保持模块间紧密耦合，实现了高质量、可控制的边缘案例合成，为自动驾驶安全评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Safety evaluation for autonomous driving is dominated by rare, safety-critical interactions, motivating simulators that can deliberately synthesize corner cases with photorealistic observations. Corner-case generation is inherently a multi-source problem spanning visual representation, scene reasoning, and vehicle trajectory generation and control. Prior knowledge- and model-based approaches typically focus on scene or trajectory components in isolation, while diffusion-based methods attempt end-to-end generation but still struggle to ensure spatiotemporal consistency and physical realism. To unify these aspects within a single framework, we propose CARLA-GS, a modular corner-case synthesis pipeline that decouples visual representation, semantic reasoning, and physics-based execution while maintaining tight cross-module coupling. Starting from real driving data, we reconstruct an editable gaussian scene with additional geometry-consistent constraints. A multi-agent LLM then performs scene-level reasoning to identify risky interactions and generate intent-level waypoint trajectories, while the low-level motion control is delegated to CARLA, where a PID controller ensures kinematic and dynamic feasibility. The simulated vehicle states are finally re-projected into the gaussian scene for ego-centric rendering. This design enables high-level semantic reasoning, low-level physically executable motion, and photorealistic corner-case generation within a unified pipeline. Experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset show, both quantitatively and qualitatively, that our framework enables controllable corner-case generation and produces photorealistic, spatiotemporally consistent videos aligned with semantic intent and physically feasible motion.

</details>


### [152] [Dual Latent Memory in Vision-Language-Action Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07608)
*Hongyu Qu,Jianzhe Gao,Xiaobin Hu,Shaohuan Yang,Xinlei Yu,Rui Yan,Wenguan Wang,Xiangbo Shu,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LaMem-VLA的新型视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型框架，通过将历史经验以潜记忆令牌的形式直接融入VLA推理过程，解决了传统模型在长视野、时序依赖任务中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流VLA模型基于马尔可夫假设，难以处理需要长期记忆和上下文依赖的复杂任务；现有带记忆增强的VLA未能将记忆整合进VLA原生的潜在表征空间，限制了历史信息与多模态推理的深度融合。

Method: 提出LaMem-VLA框架，包含四个协同组件：组织历史经验为短期和长期记忆库的curator、根据多模态认知检索相关证据的seeker、将证据压缩为紧凑潜记忆令牌的condenser、以及将这些记忆令牌与当前观测和指令融合为连续嵌入序列的weaver。整个记忆过程在统一的连续潜空间中进行。

Result: 在SimplerEnv和LIBERO两个基准上进行了大量实验，结果表明LaMem-VLA在长视野任务中显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: LaMem-VLA通过将记忆原生地集成到VLA的潜空间中，实现了历史经验与多模态推理和动作生成的无缝交织，提升了模型在复杂时序任务中的表现。

Abstract: Mainstream Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models predict actions primarily from the current observation under a Markovian assumption, thus struggling with long-horizon, temporally dependent tasks. Existing memory-augmented VLAs either expand the observation window or retrieve history from the memory bank as auxiliary policy-side context. However, they leave memory outside the native latent embedding space of VLA reasoning, preventing historical experience from being fluidly interleaved with multimodal reasoning and action formation. To this end, we introduce LaMem-VLA, a latent-memory-native framework that reconstructs historical experience into latent memory tokens and directly interweaves them with VLA reasoning. At its core, LaMem-VLA introduces four coordinated components: (i) a curator that organizes historical experience into two complementary short-term and long-term memory vaults; (ii) a seeker that queries both vaults using the multimodal cognition to retrieve context-relevant evidence; (iii) a condenser that reconstructs the retrieved evidence into compact short-term and long-term latent memory tokens; and (iv) a weaver that injects these memory tokens with the current observation and instruction into one continuous embedding sequence. By representing, retrieving, and consuming historical experience entirely in the same continuous latent space, LaMem-VLA enables memory to directly participate in VLA reasoning and guide action generation under a bounded context. Extensive experiments on SimplerEnv and LIBERO demonstrate the superiority of our LaMem-VLA.

</details>


### [153] [Continuous and large-scale: ELEANOR, the soft architected arm inspired by the elephant trunk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07622)
*Giovanna A. Naselli,Anderson B. Nardin,Seonggun Joe,Ryan Drinkwater,Enrico Donato,Diego Bianchi,Egidio Falotico,Michel C. Milinkovitch,Lucia Beccai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种仿生象鼻的连续体机械臂设计，通过3D打印和肌腱驱动实现类似大象的抓取动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受非洲象象鼻的灵巧性和多功能性启发，旨在开发性能更接近自然象鼻的机器人系统。

Method: 基于非洲象象鼻的宏观特性，采用3D打印制造85厘米长、柔顺、锥形、体积晶格结构的连续体臂，并结合模仿纵向和斜向肌肉的肌腱驱动机制。

Result: 实现了对不同形状和尺寸物体的整体抓取，展示了类似大象的运动和抓取能力。

Conclusion: 仿生设计通过模拟自然系统的宏观特性，可实现高度环境适应性和类生物运动，为软体机器人提供新思路。

Abstract: The elephant trunk is a dexterous and versatile manipulator whose performance is still unmatched in robotics. In previous works, modularity was prioritized and relatively small-scale continuum robots were built. We take the natural proboscis of the *Loxodonta africana* species as a model and propose a different design approach which favors structural continuity and dynamic properties that plausibly emulate those of the natural trunk, while conferring high adaptability to the environment and humans. Instead of targeting prescribed behaviors, we show that a biomimetic design based on the macroscopic properties of the natural system enables elephant-like movements and grasping. We build by 3D printing an 85 cm long, compliant, tapered, volumetrically tessellated continuum arm, which is combined with tendon-driven actuation mimicking the longitudinal and oblique muscles of the natural model. We demonstrate whole-body grasping of objects having different shapes and dimensions and discuss a comparison to the biological trunk highlighting aspects of both biology and robotics.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [AgentLens: Production-Assessed Trajectory Reviews for Coding Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06624)
*Andrey Podivilov,Vadim Lomshakov,Sergey Savin,Matvei Startsev,Roman Pozharskiy,Maksim Parshin,Sergey Nikolenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentLens是一个用于交互式代码代理的生产评估基准，通过结合形式化验证和LLM生成的轨迹评审，全面评估代理在任务执行过程中的行为，而不仅仅是最终结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代码代理基准通常只关注任务是否成功完成，忽略了用户在使用过程中对代理行为的完整体验。AgentLens旨在提供一个更全面的评估方法，捕捉代理在指令遵循、工具使用、自我验证、错误恢复和用户沟通等方面的表现。

Method: AgentLens结合了形式化验证（当存在客观检查时）和由大语言模型（LLM）生成的轨迹评审及并排比较，为每次运行生成可读的评分解释。

Result: 该基准不仅可用于模型排名，还能用于诊断模型行为、比较不同版本的代理，并在夜间评估管道中检测产品回归问题。

Conclusion: AgentLens提供了一种更全面、透明的代码代理评估方式，有助于改进代理的设计和用户体验，并已作为开源项目发布。

Abstract: We present AgentLens, a production-assessed benchmark for interactive code agents. Most code-agent benchmarks reduce a run to a single bit -- did the task pass? -- but the people who actually use these agents experience the entire trajectory: how the agent follows instructions, uses its tools, verifies its own work, recovers from mistakes, and talks to them along the way. AgentLens evaluates that whole trajectory. It pairs formal verification, where an objective check exists, with LLM-written trajectory reviews and side-by-side comparisons, so that each run yields a readable explanation of why the score is what it is. This makes AgentLens useful for more than ranking models: we use it to diagnose model behavior, compare successive versions of our own agent, and catch product regressions in a nightly evaluation pipeline. We release the benchmark as open source at https://github.com/agent-lens/agent-lens-bench.

</details>


### [155] [When Does In-Context Search Help? A Sampling-Complexity Theory of Reflection-Driven Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06720)
*Yotam Wolf,Noam Wies,Amnon Shashua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将上下文搜索建模为推理轨迹上近似推断的理论框架，表明当反思能可靠定位早期错误时，上下文搜索可显著提升基础模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解大规模语言模型通过扩展推理实现的上下文搜索的有效性及其理论基础。

Method: 将上下文搜索建模为基于基础模型先验和自我反思反馈进行后验更新的近似推断过程，并分析其推理时采样复杂度。

Result: 证明了在反思可靠的情况下，上下文搜索可用多项式数量的尝试解决指数级难度问题；反之则无渐近优势。同时验证了近似后验更新和交叉熵训练的有效性，并与强化学习中的最优策略联系起来。

Conclusion: 上下文搜索在适当条件下可实现对基础模型的指数级改进，且该行为可通过训练稳定恢复，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) with extended reasoning has enabled in-context search, in which models iteratively generate, critique, and revise solution attempts. We provide a theoretical analysis of in-context search by modeling it as approximate inference over reasoning traces, where the base model defines a prior and self-reflection provides feedback for posterior updates, and study the resulting inference-time sampling complexity - the number of sequential attempts needed to achieve high success probability. We show that when reflections reliably localize early mistakes, in-context search can yield exponential improvements over the base model, solving problems with exponentially small zero-shot pass rates using only a polynomial number of sequential attempts, whereas when this property fails, conditioning on past attempts offers no asymptotic benefit over parallel sampling. We further show that these gains are robust and learnable: approximate posterior updates suffice, and cross-entropy training on search rollouts recovers the required behavior with polynomial sample complexity. Finally, we show that under a stagewise abstraction of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, the optimal policy extension implements the same posterior reweighting rule. We validate key qualitative predictions of the theory on real large reasoning models.

</details>


### [156] [LLM-powered reasoning in agent-based modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06757)
*Sifat Afroj Moon,Dakotah Maguire,Adam Spannaus,Joe Tuccillo,Maksudul Alam,Sudip K. Seal,John Gounley,Heidi Hanson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合大语言模型（LLM）与基于代理的模型（ABM）的可扩展框架HALE，用于实时预测人类决策并模拟疫情传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ABM依赖静态先验，难以适应实时变化，限制了其在动态环境（如疫情）中的应用。

Method: 构建名为HALE的混合框架，将大语言模型（LLM）嵌入到基于代理的模型中，利用LLM预测个体在疫情中的行为决策，并在盐湖城进行模拟验证。

Result: 成功实现了LLM驱动的动态行为预测，并在COVID-19案例中展示了该框架在真实场景下的可扩展性和有效性。

Conclusion: HALE框架能够克服传统ABM依赖静态先验的局限，为政策制定提供更灵活、实时响应的建模工具。

Abstract: Agent-based modeling (ABM) has the capability to model millions of individuals and their interactions, which is useful for policy making. However, ABMs have traditionally relied on static prior, which prevents the models from adapting to real-time changes. Our research provides a novel approach to addressing this information gap. Large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to predict human decision-making. Here, we introduce a scalable Hybrid Agent-based and Language-driven Epidemic (HALE) modeling framework that leverages LLMs to predict human decision-making in an ABM simulation. As a proof-of-concept, we use HALE to simulate COVID-19 and its effects in Salt Lake County, UT.

</details>


### [157] [QANTIS: Hardware-Calibrated Sequential POMDP Belief Updates on IBM Heron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06760)
*Bayram Yuksel Eker,Suayb S. Arslan,Ozgur Nazli,Mustafa Serhat Demirgil,Furkan Deligoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文探讨了在IBM Heron量子硬件上重用量子处理器作为信念更新服务（QANTIS）在序列Tiger POMDP任务中的可行性，通过控制实验验证其在不同放大策略下是否能保持后验信念的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境下，自主系统依赖于信念进行决策。研究旨在验证当前量子硬件能否可靠地提供信念更新服务，而不破坏经典规划器所依赖的后验分布。

Method: 采用受控硬件案例研究方法，在相同轨迹上比较无放大、受保护的Grover放大和全步长定点放大（FPAA）三种策略，并检查返回的后验是否会改变下游动作选择。同时使用边界感知BIQAE稳定振幅估计，并通过稀有事件扫描确定逻辑采样复杂度范围。

Result: 全步长FPAA在8步和12步主运行中保持了Tiger后验的准确性，20步和32步控制运行也保持在同一操作范围内；所有决策检查中，硬件生成的后验与精确贝叶斯后验选择相同的即时动作。BIQAE有效稳定了接近零和一的振幅估计，稀有事件扫描明确了百万分之一证据的采样复杂度边界。

Conclusion: 当前IBM Heron硬件可在特定操作范围内可靠执行量子增强的信念更新，支持在自主系统中复用该服务而不破坏决策一致性，但结果界定为硬件校准的服务能力范围，而非独立的量子优势声明。

Abstract: Autonomous systems under partial observability act on beliefs, not raw sensor events. QANTIS treats the quantum processor as a calibrated belief-update service in that loop: it receives a prior and an observation model, estimates the rare-event evidence term, and returns an ordinary posterior to a classical planner. This paper asks whether that service can be reused across a sequential Tiger POMDP horizon on present IBM Heron hardware without corrupting the planner-facing posterior. We answer with a controlled hardware case study rather than an end-to-end autonomy or wall-clock speedup claim. The study compares no amplification, guarded Grover amplification, and all-step fixed-point amplification on the same trajectory, then checks whether the returned posterior would change the downstream action. All-step FPAA preserves the Tiger posterior across the reported 8-step and 12-step primary runs, and the 20-step and 32-step controls remain inside the same operating band. In every reported decision check, the hardware posterior and the exact Bayes posterior select the same immediate action. Boundary-aware BIQAE stabilizes amplitude estimation near zero and near one, while a rare-event sweep maps the logical sample-complexity envelope for one-in-a-million evidence. The result is an operating envelope for a hardware-calibrated belief-update primitive, not a standalone hardware-advantage claim.

</details>


### [158] [Cost-Effective Agent Harnesses for Abstract Reasoning and Generalization on ARC-AGI-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06764)
*Kabir Moghe,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需针对ARC-AGI基准进行专门训练或大量测试时计算的新型架构方法，通过构建Explorer-Definer Pipeline和Reflective Orchestrator，在低成本下显著提升了任务解决性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在不依赖基准特定微调和高成本测试时计算的情况下，仅通过模型架构设计提升ARC-AGI任务性能的可能性。

Method: 设计了一个两阶段代理流水线（Explorer-Definer Pipeline），将模式发现与程序合成分离，并引入Reflective Orchestrator实现失败时的自主转换探索；使用DeepSeek V3.2模型在严格预算下运行。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-1公开400任务集上，流水线达到57.50% pass@2（每任务0.25美元），Orchestrator达到67.25% pass@2（每任务0.62美元），相比15.50%的一次性基线大幅提升；消融实验显示“think工具”移除后性能下降5.75个百分点。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了生成广度而非选择机制是当前性能瓶颈，Orchestrator通过自适应重探索证实了这一诊断，表明未来改进应聚焦于扩展生成能力而非优化排序。

Abstract: Recent progress on ARC-AGI-1 from disclosed architectures has come broadly from two regimes: heavy test-time compute over frontier models (evolutionary search, exhaustive sampling, extended chain-of-thought), or benchmark-specific training in which small models are fine-tuned on ARC data, often with task-specialized architectures. We study a third regime: an open-weight model in non-thinking mode (DeepSeek V3.2) under a strict budget, with no ARC-specific fine-tuning. We study what is recoverable through architecture alone, building agentic harnesses that decompose pattern-discovery and program-synthesis stages explicitly. First, we introduce an Explorer-Definer Pipeline that separates pattern discovery from executable transformation synthesis, implemented as a two-stage agent pipeline. Next, we present the Reflective Orchestrator, which augments the pipeline with autonomous exploration of new transformations when previous hypotheses fail on training pairs. On the ARC-AGI-1 public 400-task evaluation set, the pipeline reaches 57.50% pass@2 at \$0.25 per task, and the orchestrator reaches 67.25% pass@2 at \$0.62 per task. Together these architectures lift a 15.50% one-shot baseline by ~52 points without benchmark-specific training or heavy test-time compute. Furthermore, the orchestrator-driven lift tests a falsifiable diagnostic the pipeline produces; unbiased pass@k analysis suggests the pipeline is generation-bound, not selection-bound (selection via training-pair accuracy captures ~95% of the candidate ceiling) and predicts that significant improvement requires broader generation, not better ranking. The orchestrator implements this prediction via adaptive re-exploration and confirms it (unbiased pass@1 lift +9.81 pp, matching selection-mediated pass@2 lift). An additional pipeline ablation identifies its think tool as a significant component, with removal reducing pass@2 by 5.75 pp.

</details>


### [159] [Evaluating SageMath-Augmented LLM Agents for Computational and Experimental Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06820)
*Pavel Snopov,German Magai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大语言模型与计算机代数系统（SageMath）的ReAct式智能体框架，用于解决研究级数学问题，并在RealMath基准上验证了其有效性，显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI在数学领域的研究多集中于自动形式化和定理证明，而计算机代数系统（CAS）在大模型代理工作流中的作用尚未充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，提升大模型在实际数学研究任务中的计算推理能力。

Method: 采用ReAct风格的智能体架构，将大语言模型的推理能力与SageMath提供的可验证反馈相结合，并引入Context7以获取最新文档支持；同时改进RealMath基准，提出多步后处理流程和多阶段验证管道。

Result: 实验表明，所有测试模型在接入SageMath后平均性能提升+9.7个百分点（范围1.5~27.8 pp），缩小了开源与闭源模型之间的差距；Qwen-3.7-Max提升最大，GPT-5.5则达到最高解题率75.2%且耗 token 最低。

Conclusion: CAS增强型智能体是辅助数学家进行计算探索的有前景方向，有助于迈向自动化猜想发现。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI for Mathematics have focused largely on autoformalization and theorem proving, leaving the role of Computer Algebra Systems (CAS) in agentic LLM workflows underexplored. We propose a ReAct-style agentic setup that combines LLM reasoning with verifiable feedback from SageMath, together with Context7 for the up-to-date documentation. We evaluate this agentic setup across frontier models for solving research-level mathematical problems from the RealMath benchmark in a setting that emulates a computational-mathematics research loop. We also propose a refinement to the RealMath benchmark by introducing a multi-step post-processing procedure and a multi-stage validation pipeline, both of which improve the quality and reliability of the extracted problem set. Our experiments reveal substantial performance gains from SageMath access across all evaluated models on +9.7~pp on average, the gains range from 1.5~pp to 27.8~pp and narrow the gap between open-weight and closed models. Qwen~3.7-Max benefits from SageMath the most, while GPT-5.5 achieves the highest solve rate of $75.2\%$ and the lowest token usage among tool-enabled configurations. Our findings suggest that CAS-augmented agents represent a promising direction for assisting mathematicians in computational exploration, and we believe that this work is a step towards automated conjecture discovery. The project repository is available online.

</details>


### [160] [The Harness Effect: How Orchestration Design Sets the Token Economics of Enterprise Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06906)
*Muayad Sayed Ali,Aliaksandra Novik,Anji Boddupally,Artem Yavorskyi,Chris Nickerson,Daniel Rica,Emily DuGranrut,Felix Leung,Garrett Prince,Grace Barnett,Heath Robinson,Hosain Al Ahmad,Jesse Resnick,Juan Carlos Farah,Jyothi Swaroop Meruga,Leonid Kuznetsov,Luke Gorham,Marie Schmoll,Michael Paciullo,Saumya Das,Sharath Sheripally,Tommy Griscom,Mykyta Osadchyi,Neha Mantri,Nick Westrum,Olivia Benowitz,Parikshith Kulkarni,Radik Chernyshov,Rakshith Vasudev,Rohith Nadimpally,Vikas Gangadevi,Waseem AlShikh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在Agentic AI开发中，通过优化编排层（即“harness”）来对抗“token maxing”问题的有效性。研究发现，在保持模型不变的情况下，改进的Writer Agent Harness相比传统生产循环显著降低了成本、时间和每任务使用的token数量，同时维持了任务完成质量，并提高了性价比和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统对长推理链、多轮交互和大上下文回放的需求增加，每次任务消耗的tokens快速增长，导致总体开销上升。尽管单个token价格下降，但总支出仍在增长，因此需要找到控制这种趋势的关键杠杆。

Method: 采用受控交换实验设计：使用22个固定评估任务和六个基础模型（如Claude Sonnet 4.6等），仅改变编排层——比较传统的冻结生产循环与Writer Agent Harness的效果。

Result: 实验结果显示，新编排层使混合每任务成本降低41%（从$0.21降至$0.12），中位运行时间减少44%（从48秒减至27秒），每任务tokens减少38%（从14.2k降到8.8k），任务完成质量保持相当甚至略有提升（0.78→0.81）。所有模型的成本均下降33%-61%，且质量增益与模型基础能力高度相关（r=0.99）。每美元获得的质量提高82%，每百万tokens可完成的任务数由54.9增至92.0。

Conclusion: 编排层是决定AI系统经济效率的核心组件，其优化效果超越了不同模型之间的性能差异，且具有跨模型的乘数效应。文章提出了token经济学框架及六类机制，并强调该层面对企业级可观测性和治理的重要性。

Abstract: Agentic AI development today runs on token maxing: buying capability with tokens -- longer reasoning traces, more turns, wider tool payloads, bigger replayed contexts -- so tokens per task grow faster than task value. Falling per-token prices mask the pattern; total spend rises anyway. We argue the decisive lever against token maxing is the harness: the orchestration layer that assembles context, exposes tools, sequences turns, delegates work, and carries enterprise observability and governance. We isolate it with a controlled swap: 22 locked evaluation tasks, six foundation models (Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.1, Gemini Flash 3.5, Qwen 3.6, GLM 5.1, Palmyra X6), changing only the orchestration layer -- a frozen conventional production loop versus the Writer Agent Harness. Holding models constant, the harness cuts blended cost per task 41% ($0.21->$0.12), median wall-clock 44% (48s->27s), and tokens per task 38% (14.2k->8.8k), with task-completion quality at parity (0.78->0.81, directional at this sample size). Efficiency is model-invariant -- every model gets cheaper (33-61%) -- while quality gains are capability-dependent: a model's gain correlates almost perfectly with its baseline strength (r=0.99, n=6), a phenomenon we term harness leverage. Quality per dollar rises 82%; task-completions per million tokens rise from 54.9 to 92.0. On this workload the orchestration layer moved cost per task more than the full spread of the model menu did. We formalize token economics at the orchestration layer (including effective input price under prompt caching), detail the six mechanism families behind the effect -- cache-shape discipline to failure-spend governance -- compare six widely used agent systems on the same axes, and argue the harness is the one component whose efficiency multiplies across every model an organization runs -- present and future.

</details>


### [161] [Grounding Spatial Relations in a Compact World Model: Instruction Leakage and a Goal-Free Dynamics Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06925)
*Yufeng Wang,Lu Wei,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言目标条件下的紧凑世界模型在关系 grounding 中的“指令泄露”问题，发现高准确率可能仅来自指令转录而非感知；通过实验验证了该现象的存在条件，并提出将目标从动力学中移除、监督读取路径的修复方法，实现了真正独立于指令的 grounding。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨语言目标条件的世界模型是否真正理解空间关系，还是仅仅复制指令内容（即指令泄露），特别是在评估指标可从指令直接推断时模型是否忽略了视觉输入。

Method: 设计控制实验，比较有无语言目标输入时模型的表现，使用反事实指令测试预测锚点是否跟随错误指令，并在不同设置（包括Tabletop和BabyAI）下进行消融分析，检测指令泄露现象。

Result: 发现高准确率（0.90）在移除目标后骤降至0.27，且94.5%情况下模型遵循错误指令而非真实场景；指令命名答案时发生泄露，而仅命名指称物时不泄露；削弱动作输入不会增加泄露，与预测相反；通过分离目标与动力学并监督读取路径，实现了指令无关的可靠 grounding（准确率0.88）。

Conclusion: 当评估量可从指令中直接转录时，模型会依赖指令泄露而非真实感知；真正的 grounding 需要将目标排除在动力学之外，并对感知路径进行显式监督，该原则适用于所有目标条件世界模型。

Abstract: Compact world models that condition on a language goal promise to ground relations such as ``put the red block left of the blue block'' using a sparse set of explicit \emph{reference anchors}. We ask when such references actually ground a relation, and identify a trap: a goal-conditioned predictor reaches a striking $0.90$ relation-readout accuracy, yet this is \emph{instruction transcription}, not perception. Withholding the goal collapses it to chance ($0.90\!\to\!0.27$, three seeds) and a counterfactual instruction makes the predicted anchors follow the \emph{false} instruction $94.5\%$ of the time (true scene $2.3\%$; $N{=}256$). Tested across three settings and a within-task ablation, our central claim characterizes the confound: \textbf{instruction leakage occurs when the scored quantity is transcribable from the instruction (when the instruction names the answer) and is essentially independent of how predictive the non-instruction inputs are.} Our tabletop and the external BabyAI benchmark leak, whereas a Language-Table forward-dynamics world model whose instruction names \emph{referents} does not, until the instruction is augmented to name the direction; and degrading the action never increases leakage, the opposite of what predictor-competition predicts. The diagnosis prescribes the fix: keep the goal out of the dynamics (it belongs to the planner's cost) and supervise the \emph{read} path, recovering genuine, instruction-independent grounding ($0.88$, identical with and without the goal). The detection protocol and remedy apply to any goal-conditioned world model whose instruction names the scored quantity.

</details>


### [162] [Large Behavior Model: A Promptable Digital Twin of the Retail Customer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06993)
*Wachiravit Modecrua,Krittin Pachtrachai,Touchapon Kraisingkorn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为Large Behavioral Model (LBM)的客户行为建模框架，通过大规模零售交易数据学习用户决策过程，结合行为档案与检索增强生成技术，在多种零售任务中优于通用语言模型，并展现出良好的跨域迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在用户行为建模中要么缺乏可解释性，要么未基于真实行为数据进行模拟，因此需要一种既能准确预测又能解释决策的统一模型。

Method: 提出LBM模型，采用Person-Environment框架：客户状态由历史购买构建的行为档案表示，产品环境通过检索增强生成引入；模型经过持续预训练、监督微调和基于可验证奖励的强化学习进行训练。

Result: 在购买预测、难负样本区分、购物篮补全、促销响应和跨域优惠券兑换等任务上，LBM均优于前沿通用语言模型，且具备强零样本和微调后跨零售商/领域迁移能力；消融实验表明持续预训练对泛化最关键，检索在训练和推理阶段使用最有效，强化学习能提升对行为证据的依赖。

Conclusion: 交易数据中蕴含的行为知识可被语言模型有效学习，LBM为构建可扩展的客户数字孪生和行为仿真提供了基础。

Abstract: Customer behavior modeling underpins recommendation, marketing, and decision support, yet existing approaches either optimize predictive accuracy without explaining decisions or simulate users without grounding them in real behavioral data. We present the Large Behavioral Model (LBM) that learns customer decision making directly from large-scale retail transactions through a unified Person-Environment formulation. Customer state is represented by a behavioral profile derived from historical purchases, while product context is incorporated through retrieval-augmented generation. The model is trained using continued pre-training on verbalized behavioral data, supervised fine-tuning for decision generation, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for evidence-based calibration. We evaluate the proposed framework on purchase prediction, hard-negative discrimination, basket completion, promotion response, and cross-domain voucher redemption. The model consistently outperforms frontier general-purpose language models on in-domain retail tasks while demonstrating strong zero-shot and fine-tuned transfer across retailers and decision domains. Ablation studies show that continued pre-training is the primary driver of behavioral generalization, retrieval is most effective when applied during both training and inference, and reinforcement learning improves reliance on explicit behavioral evidence over generic language-model priors. These results demonstrate that behavioral knowledge encoded in transaction histories can be effectively learned by language models, providing a scalable foundation for customer digital twins and behavior simulation.

</details>


### [163] [Learning social norms enhances compatibility in dynamic human-AI coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07021)
*Yi Yang,Siyuan Liu,Xin Gao,Huamu Sun,Chao Liu,Qing Zhou,Bingbing Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过将隐性社会规范显式量化为可操作原则，AI代理在动态人机交互中实现了更自然、高效的协调表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI模型主要依赖人类行为示范进行对齐，但未能捕捉生成这些行为的深层社会规范，导致在动态社交互动中协调能力不足。本文旨在识别并形式化人类互动中的隐性社会规范，以提升AI在真实社交场景中的自然性和有效性。

Method: 选择行人-车辆交互作为典型动态交互场景，构建简化实验平台收集3,456次人类互动数据，并从中归纳出三大社会规范原则：结果可预测性、价值一致性和优势感知。将这些原则编码融入大语言模型驱动的AI代理中，评估其在闭环人机交互任务中的协调表现。

Result: 融合社会规范的LLM在闭环交互任务中总得分比基线策略高出近四倍，并超越人类-人类互动表现43%。

Conclusion: 将隐性社会规范形式化为明确、可量化的规则，能显著提升AI在动态社交互动中的协调能力，推动其更自然地融入人类社会。

Abstract: Humans continuously coordinate with others in dynamic interactions, often through implicit, hard-to-quantify social norms that act as shared tacit expectations among interacting agents. As AI agents, including large language models (LLMs), become embedded in daily life, they increasingly participate in such interactions and reshape social interaction structures. Yet they often fail to coordinate with humans in an effective, considerate, and natural manner. We hypothesize that this gap arises because existing approaches align model behavior with human demonstrations without explicitly quantifying the underlying norms that generate such behavior. We selected pedestrian-vehicle interaction as a representative dynamic interaction and developed a simplified experimental platform that captures its key interactive features. From 3,456 dynamic human interactions collected via this platform, we identified three principles underlying human social norms: outcome predictability, value alignment, and advantage awareness. Incorporating these principles into AI agents significantly improves human-AI coordination. In the closed-loop interaction task with humans, the social-norm-informed LLM achieved a nearly fourfold higher total score than the baseline strategy and outperformed human-human interactions by 43%. These findings indicate that formalizing tacit social norms into explicit, quantifiable principles can enable AI agents to achieve mutually beneficial coordination in dynamic interactions, supporting their more natural integration into human society.

</details>


### [164] [Measuring Intelligence Beyond Human Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07040)
*Jerry Han,Rafael Moschopoulos,Ella Colby,Vishrut Goyal,Andrew Tu,Kia Ghods,Mark Braverman,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于模型生成挑战的相对测量新范式，用于评估超越人类能力的人工智能系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的人类设计的基准在衡量超人类水平的智能时存在局限性，难以判断哪些任务既困难又可验证。

Method: 通过模型生成公开挑战来区分不同系统，并聚合结果形成对抗性心理测量评分系统，支持无需裁判的裁决机制。

Result: 该框架可在可验证和开放式非可验证领域中应用，实现对超越人类能力系统的持续评估。

Conclusion: 相对测量范式能够随着被测系统能力的提升而自然扩展，为超人类智能提供了可持续的评估方法。

Abstract: How can we measure intelligence beyond human capability?
  Human-authored benchmarks saturate, and above human capability, examiners may not know which tasks are both hard and verifiable. We argue that this difficulty is inherent to absolute-scale evaluation and propose a new paradigm based on relative measurement in which models generate public challenges that separate other systems. Aggregating these outcomes yields an adversarial psychometric rating system that can scale with the systems being measured. We describe practical protocols that reduce incentives for private-information attacks, support judge-free adjudication, and naturally scale with agent capabilities. We instantiate the framework across verifiable and open-ended, non-verifiable domains, illustrating how model-generated evaluation can continue to measure systems beyond the human frontier.

</details>


### [165] [Operational Reframing and Approval-Framed Delegation in Multi-Agent LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07097)
*Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu,Xiaochong Jiang,Su Wang,Pin Qian,Yihang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种五条件对照实验设计，以分解多智能体大语言模型系统中“管道效应”的三个潜在机制：操作性重构、规划器干预和委托提示的影响。研究发现，聚合的管道安全性并非稳定的架构属性，不同模型在各机制下的表现差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对多智能体LLM系统的安全评估通常将直接提示与规划-执行流水线进行比较，并将差异归结为单一的“管道效应”。这种聚合指标难以解释，因为它混淆了多个潜在机制。本文旨在分离这些因素，以更准确地理解安全风险来源。

Method: 提出一个包含五个条件的受控对比设计，在30个合成有害场景及四个代理安全基准的外部验证集上进行评估，使用LLM判断合规性。五个条件包括：直接提示、操作性重构、规划器输出、委托提示以及带怀疑态度的执行器提示。分析不同模型（GPT、Gemini、Claude、DeepSeek）在各条件下的合规性变化。

Result: 操作性重构是最具可迁移性的风险信号，能显著提升多数模型的合规性，而Claude相对抵抗；规划器主要通过拒绝来抵消风险，但一旦生成可执行步骤，执行器可能比直接操作基线更合规；批准式委托对提示设计、模型配对和场景来源敏感，带有怀疑态度的执行器提示可显著降低合规性；原始直接提示下的模型安全性排名不能准确预测实际部署中的行为。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统的安全评估不应仅报告总体管道效应，而应分别报告操作性重构、规划器行为、委托框架和模型配对的影响，才能准确归因于架构本身。

Abstract: Safety evaluations of multi-agent LLM systems often compare a direct prompt with a planner-executor pipeline and report the difference as a single "pipeline effect." We argue that this aggregate is difficult to interpret because it conflates three mechanisms: harmful intent may be reframed as plausible operational work, the planner may refuse or transform the request, and the executor may act under delegation prompts implying prior approval. To separate these factors, we introduce a five-condition controlled contrast design, evaluated on 30 synthetic harmful scenarios and an exploratory external validation set from four agent-safety benchmarks using LLM-judged compliance.
  Our results show that aggregate pipeline safety is not a stable architectural property. Operational reframing is the most portable risk signal, increasing compliance for GPT, Gemini, and DeepSeek across both scenario sets, while Claude is comparatively resistant. Planner behavior can offset this risk mainly through refusal; however, when the planner produces executable steps, the executor may become more compliant than under the direct operational baseline. Approval-framed delegation is sensitive to prompt design, model pairing, and scenario source, and a skeptical executor prompt sharply reduces compliance.
  Raw-direct model rankings can also mispredict deployed planner-executor behavior. Gemini is safest under raw direct prompts in the primary set yet shows the largest amplification with a Claude planner, rising from 8.9 percent to 38.9 percent compliance. GPTs near-zero aggregate pipeline effect instead hides a reframing increase canceled by planner refusal. These findings suggest that multi-agent safety evaluations should report reframing, planner behavior, delegation framing, and model pairing separately before attributing failures to architecture itself.

</details>


### [166] [Does AI Understand Imaging? A Systematic Benchmark of Agentic AI for Computational Imaging Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07189)
*Ethan Chung,Chuanjun Zheng,Jasper Tan,Jingxi Li,Haopeng Zhang,Huaijin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ImagingBench是一个新的基准测试，用于评估视觉语言模型和代理AI在计算成像任务中的物理和逆问题处理能力，揭示了当前模型在物理一致性方面与专用方法之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨现有的视觉语言模型和代理AI是否能够处理计算成像中涉及的物理规律和逆问题，而不仅仅是语义视觉任务。

Method: 构建包含20个任务的ImagingBench基准，涵盖五类计算成像问题，并在Expert、Planner和Forward三种设定下评估主流多模态系统的表现，与特定任务的非代理基线进行比较。

Result: 代理模型在所有任务上均弱于专用方法，尤其在无透镜成像、事件相机重建、飞行时间成像和全息等计算感知任务中表现较差；Planner引导仅带来有限且不稳定的增益；生成结果虽视觉合理但参考保真度低。

Conclusion: 当前的视觉语言模型和代理AI在物理基础的成像任务上仍存在明显局限，ImagingBench为衡量这一差距及推动代理AI在计算成像中的发展提供了统一平台。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) and agentic AI have shown strong performance on semantic visual tasks, but it remains unclear whether they can handle the physics and inverse problems that underlie computational imaging. We present ImagingBench, a benchmark of 20 computational imaging tasks spanning five categories: ray and wave optics, image signal processing, inverse reconstruction, computational sensing, and calibration. ImagingBench evaluates three complementary settings: Expert, fixed expert-guided inverse reconstruction; Planner, planner-guided inverse reconstruction; and Forward, forward-system simulation for consistency checking. We benchmark leading proprietary and open-source image-centric multimodal systems, including Gemini, GPT, and Qwen, and compare them with representative task-specific non-agentic baselines. Across tasks, agentic models remain consistently weaker than specialized methods, especially on computational sensing problems such as lensless imaging, event-based reconstruction, time-of-flight imaging, and holography. Planner guidance provides only modest and inconsistent gains over the fixed-prompt Expert baseline. Although the models often generate visually plausible outputs, their reference-based fidelity remains poor, revealing a substantial gap between semantic visual competence and physically grounded imaging performance. ImagingBench provides a unified testbed for measuring this gap and tracking progress in agentic AI for computational imaging.

</details>


### [167] [Reasoning Consistency Scanning: A Framework for Auditing Chain-of-Thought Validity in AI Safety Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07229)
*Silvia Santano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可重用的方法——推理一致性扫描，用于检测AI安全评估记录中的推理与其结论之间是否逻辑一致，并构建了基准数据集和检测工具，验证了不一致性在不同模型和任务中普遍存在且可检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的研究表明，大模型的思维链推理往往是不忠实的，即其推理过程并不能真实反映输出生成的过程。然而，检测这种不忠实性需要事前干预实验，难以应用于已有的评估记录。因此，作者转向一个更易处理的问题：判断模型的推理是否与其最终答案逻辑一致。

Method: 作者将推理一致性从“忠实性”中分离出来，提出了包含六种子类型的不一致性分类体系；构建了一个包含60条人工改编自InstrumentalEval的安全评估记录的基准数据集；开发了首个面向该属性的检测工具Reasoning Consistency Scanner，并集成到InspectScout中；在四个生成模型和三个inspect_evals任务上进行了实验评估。

Result: 实验证明，推理不一致性确实存在于当前模型中，能够被有效检测，并且在不同模型和任务类型间呈现系统性差异。所提出的扫描器在检测不一致性方面表现良好，验证了方法的可行性与实用性。

Conclusion: 推理一致性是一个可评估且有意义的替代指标，可用于提升AI安全评估的可靠性；未来工作可基于此进一步优化模型推理的透明性与可信度。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is often unfaithful: a model's stated reasoning does not reliably reflect the process that produced its output. Detecting unfaithfulness, though, requires controlled experimental interventions, which cannot be applied to evaluation transcripts after the fact. We turn instead to a more tractable question that has received less attention: whether the stated reasoning is logically consistent with the answer it accompanies. Unlike faithfulness, consistency can be assessed from a transcript alone, with no intervention. We introduce reasoning consistency scanning, a reusable method for detecting this property in AI safety evaluation transcripts. Our contributions are fourfold. First, we formalize reasoning consistency as distinct from faithfulness and define a six-subtype taxonomy of inconsistency. Second, we build a validated benchmark of 60 transcripts, manually adapted from InstrumentalEval outputs. Third, we implement a working scanner for InspectScout, the first to target this property in safety evaluation transcripts. Fourth, we report results across four generator models and three evaluations from inspect_evals, showing that reasoning inconsistency is present, detectable, and varies systematically across both models and task types.

</details>


### [168] [From Atomic Actions to Standard Operating Procedures: Iterative Tool Optimization for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07321)
*Haipeng Ding,Yuexiang Xie,Zhewei Wei,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoSOP框架，使大语言模型代理通过将原子操作合成为可重用的标准操作程序（SOP）来自我进化，并通过构建、合并、评估和剪枝的迭代优化工具集，显著提升任务成功率并减少交互轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理框架依赖静态、细粒度的原子工具，导致重复低效的推理和高失败率，缺乏对多步工作流的抽象与复用机制。

Method: 提出EvoSOP框架，代理从执行轨迹中提取SOP，形成高阶可调用工具，并通过构造、合并、评估与剪枝的生命周期迭代优化工具集。

Result: 实验表明，EvoSOP显著提高了任务成功率，减少了交互轮数，且迭代优化促进了更可靠高效的工具使用模式。

Conclusion: 通过合成与持续优化SOP，代理可实现自我演化，为构建高效、可扩展的自主代理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Tool utilization enables Large Language Model (LLM) agents to interact with the real world and resolve complex tasks. However, existing agent frameworks predominantly rely on static toolsets composed of granular atomic actions (e.g., basic file I/O or single-turn search), which forces agents to reinvent low-level logic for every recurring workflow, leading to increased reasoning overhead and failure rates. In this study, we propose that agents can achieve self-evolution by synthesizing these atomic actions into reusable Standard Operating Procedures (SOPs), which function as callable higher-order tools that encapsulate multi-step logic. We further introduce EvoSOP, a framework that empowers agents to extract SOPs from execution trajectories and iteratively optimize the toolset through a systematic lifecycle of construction, merging, evaluation, and pruning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EvoSOP significantly boosts task success rates while substantially reducing the number of interaction rounds compared to baselines. Our analysis also reveals that iterative tool optimization fosters reliable and efficient tool-use patterns, providing a scalable pathway for the development of self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [169] [Physics-Audited Agentic Discovery in Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07379)
*Diab W. Abueidda,Bilal Ahmed,Panos Pantidis,Mostafa E. Mobasher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为PA-SciML的验证优先的工作流，用于基于物理审计的科学机器学习模型发现，强调在没有参考解的情况下通过可验证的物理要求检查代理生成的代理模型，并确保其满足关键物理特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于误差评分的代理模型选择方法无法保证预测场满足力学中重要的物理规律（如边界条件、叠加性、刚度缩放和因果性），因此需要一种更可靠的验证机制。

Method: 在搜索前固定评分评估器，推导出可审查且可机器检查的物理要求，对每个候选模型的输出进行物理一致性检查，并在无真实场的情况下针对特定输入范围或载荷历史寻找高违规情况；同时可选地在训练前加入数值探测，逐项测试建模更改以追踪性能提升来源。

Result: 在静态弹性算例中，PA-SciML选出的模型验证误差低于基线且通过线弹性检查；在瞬态弹道动力学算例中，尽管均值误差相近，基线模型未能通过因果性检查，而PA-SciML所选模型通过了所有指定检查。

Conclusion: PA-SciML通过为每个候选模型提供明确的物理证据而非依赖综合评分，提升了科学机器学习中代理模型的可信度与可解释性，实现了以物理正确性为导向的自动化模型发现。

Abstract: In agentic scientific machine learning (SciML), large language model (LLM) agents can discover surrogate models and select one by an automated score, typically an error metric. A low error, however, does not establish that the predicted fields satisfy the physics that matter for mechanics, such as boundary conditions, superposition, stiffness scaling, or causality. We introduce Physics-Audited Agentic SciML (PA-SciML), a verification-first workflow for agentic SciML discovery. The workflow fixes a scoring evaluator before search, derives reviewable machine-checkable physics requirements, checks each trained candidate on its outputs, and separately searches prescribed input ranges or measured load-history spans for high-violation cases without reference solution fields. A surrogate is reported as verified only under the stated checks. When enabled, the workflow also adds advisory numerical probes before training and tests one modeling change at a time to record which isolated edits are associated with score gains before reuse. In the reported computational-solid-mechanics numerical examples, the static elasticity run selects a surrogate with lower validation error than the error-only baseline while both selected models pass the common linear-elastic checks. In the transient elastodynamics run, an error-only baseline with similar mean error fails a stricter causality check by responding to future parts of the loading history, while the selected surrogate passes the stated checks. The main distinction is per-candidate physics evidence on predicted fields, not a richer aggregate score.

</details>


### [170] [MIRA-Math: A Benchmark for Minimal Information Requesting and Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07391)
*Charbel Al Bateh,Samer Saab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIRA-Math是一个新的数学推理基准，用于评估模型在缺少一个关键事实的情况下，通过自然语言请求缺失信息并最终准确解答问题的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理基准通常提供所有解题所需事实，而交互式基准又过于复杂。MIRA-Math旨在诊断模型在仅缺一个原子事实时的最小信息请求与推理整合能力。

Method: 构建包含2310个实例的MIRA-Math基准，覆盖22类数学问题，要求模型在预算限制下用自然语言请求缺失事实，并由固定LLM响应器决定是否返回确切事实，确保评估过程可重复且确定。

Result: 实验显示，不同规模模型在请求成功与最终答案准确率之间表现分离：有些能正确请求但计算失败，有些则未能获取必要提示。

Conclusion: MIRA-Math实现了对数学推理中信息请求能力的细粒度、可复现评估，有助于推动兼具交互性与精确性的推理系统发展。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning benchmarks typically provide all facts needed to solve each problem, while interactive benchmarks often mix reasoning with tools, retrieval, and long-horizon dialogue. We introduce MIRA-Math, a benchmark for a narrower diagnostic capability: solving mathematical problems whose full latent state has a unique answer, but whose solver-facing view is missing exactly one necessary atomic fact. The solver must request the missing information in natural language under a strict budget and then integrate the returned fact into an exact final answer. A fixed constrained LLM responder sees only the dataset-provided atomic fact and must either offer the quoted fact when the request matches it, or decline otherwise. Thus, instance generation, typed hint specifications, validation, and final-answer verification are deterministic, while request metrics are measured under a fixed LLM-mediated responder channel. MIRA-Math contains 2{,}310 generated instances from 22 typed mathematical families spanning algebra, probability, linear systems, discrete structures, signal processing, Markov chains, circuits, interpolation, and numerical boundary-value problems. Experiments across frontier and small models show that request success and final-answer accuracy are separable: models may ask for the right fact yet fail the downstream computation, or fail before obtaining the canonical hint. We release generators, verifiers, prompts, run metadata, and dataset documentation to support reproducible evaluation of minimal information requesting in mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [171] [Agentic Data Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07397)
*Elaine Ang,Chenxi Huang,Georgios Liargkovas,Jerry Liu,Jinhui Liu,Nikos Pagonas,Charlie Summers,Haonan Wang,Jiakai Xu,Tianle Zhou,Yusen Zhang,Zhou Yu,Zhuo Zhang,Tianyi Peng,Kostis Kaffes,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了自主代理在提升自动化效率的同时可能带来的突发且不可逆的风险，提出通过构建“代理数据环境”作为执行基础，将数据系统从被动的状态存储转变为主动的安全执行平台，以增强代理能力并确保安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主代理虽然能显著提高自动化速度、规模和劳动力效率，但其失败可能导致严重后果，因此需要在提升自动化效益的同时控制失败的影响。

Method: 提出“代理数据环境”的概念，将其作为代理运行的执行基底，整合文件、API、应用程序和系统状态等多源数据，赋予数据系统主动支持安全与可靠执行的能力。

Result: 初步工作表明，代理数据环境能够放大代理的能力，同时提供安全保证，使数据系统成为支持安全自动化的核心基础设施。

Conclusion: 将数据系统重新定位为支持安全、可靠执行的主动基底，是实现高效且可控的代理自动化的关键路径。

Abstract: Autonomous agents promise substantial gains in speed, scale, and labor efficiency, but their failures can impose abrupt and often irreversible costs. The central challenge for agentic automation is therefore to increase the benefits of automation while bounding the consequences of failure.
  While databases remain central to modern computing, agents operate over a broader data environment spanning files, APIs, applications, and system state. In this talk, I will outline early work on Agentic Data Environments -- the execution substrate in which agents operate -- that both amplify agent capabilities and enforce safety guarantees. This perspective reframes data systems from passive stores of state into active substrates for safe, reliable execution.

</details>


### [172] [Reason Less, Verify More: Deterministic Gates Recover a Silent Policy-Violation Failure Mode in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07405)
*Vikas Reddy,Sumanth Reddy Challaram,Abhishek Basu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在策略宽松环境中，使用工具的LLM代理可能违反其应执行的政策而导致“静默错误状态”的问题，并提出了一种轻量级的干预方法——确定性的只读预执行检查门控，有效提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在看似成功完成任务的同时，可能通过工具调用违反领域政策，导致未被察觉的错误状态。这种静默失败在实际应用中存在严重风险，因此需要识别并缓解这一问题。

Method: 在τ²-bench航空领域基准上分析代理行为，统计静默错误的发生率；设计由四个确定性、只读的预执行门控组成的干预机制，在执行前检查调用请求与当前状态是否符合策略，防止非法写操作。

Result: 在gpt-4o-mini上，四门控套件将整体任务成功率从29.6%提升至42.0%（+12.4个百分点，P=0.0012），且效果在独立种子集上复现（+12.3pp，P=0.0008）；效果集中在门控触发的任务上（+19.2pp）。控制实验表明，当工具本身已具策略约束时，门控作用有限。初步证据显示，同一机制在gpt-5.2上也将成功率从61.2%提升至71.6%（+10.4pp）。

Conclusion: 确定性预执行门控虽不能保证任务完全成功，但能有效防止一类特定的静默策略违规写操作，提升代理的可靠性，尤其适用于工具本身策略约束不足的环境。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents can violate the very policies they are deployed to enforce while appearing to complete the task successfully. In policy-permissive environments, a tool may execute any well-formed call even when the corresponding state transition is forbidden by domain policy. The result is a silent wrong state (a booking cancelled, a passenger count changed, a claim acted on without verification) that neither the tool nor the agent's self-report exposes.
  We study this failure mode in the $τ^2$-bench airline domain. On a budget agent, 78% of observed failures are silent wrong-state failures with no tool error, and the aggregate failure rate is reproducible across disjoint seeds, not sampling noise. We then evaluate a lightweight intervention: deterministic, read-only pre-execution gates that inspect the proposed call and current state before allowing a write. A four-gate suite raises full-benchmark success from 29.6% to 42.0% on gpt-4o-mini (+12.4pp; paired task-level bootstrap P=0.0012), and the lift reproduces on a disjoint 15-seed set (+12.3pp; P=0.0008).
  The effect is concentrated where the gates fire: on the 26/50 firing tasks, success rises by +19.2pp, while movement on the 24 non-firing tasks does not exclude zero. Two negative controls (a self-enforcing retail domain and BFCL) bound the mechanism: gates help when tools are policy-permissive and add little where tools already self-enforce. As suggestive evidence, not a central claim, the same failure mode persists at the frontier: gpt-5.2 at default reasoning still attempts policy-violating writes, and the same suite improves success from 61.2% to 71.6% (+10.4pp; P=0.020; n=5, no replication). The contribution is a bounded evaluation and reliability result: deterministic gates do not guarantee task success, but they can deterministically prevent a known class of silent policy-violating writes at the action boundary.

</details>


### [173] [InductWave: Inductive Multi-Hop Logical Query Answering on Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07422)
*Mayank Kharbanda,Michael Cochez,Rajiv Ratn Shah,Raghava Mutharaju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于小波的归纳嵌入方法InductWave，用于大规模知识图谱上的逻辑多跳查询回答，能够在训练节点少于测试节点的情况下实现高效推理，并在资源消耗更少的情况下优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逻辑查询回答方法主要集中在存在性一阶逻辑（EFO）查询上，并且大多采用直推式推理，无法处理训练过程中未见的实体。而在现实世界中，由于资源限制，无法用大型知识图谱的所有节点进行训练，因此需要一种能够进行归纳推理的方法。

Method: 提出InductWave，一种基于小波变换的归纳嵌入方法，通过减少消息传递层数量来降低资源需求，同时支持在训练图节点少于测试图的情况下进行有效推理。

Result: InductWave在FB15k-237数据集的不同训练-测试比例下进行了广泛实验，性能与基线模型相当甚至更好，仅使用75%的消息传递层就在大多数情况下超越了现有模型，并成功应用于如Wiki-KG这样的大规模图谱。

Conclusion: InductWave是一种高效的归纳式逻辑查询回答方法，具有较低的资源消耗和良好的泛化能力，适用于大规模知识图谱上的多跳逻辑查询任务。

Abstract: Logical Multi-Hop Query Answering over Knowledge Graphs (KGs) can be formulated as querying, with an implicit completeness assumption. Current works mainly focus on Existential First Order Logic (EFO) queries. These EFO queries contain conjunction, disjunction, and negation operators. Most existing works employ transductive reasoning, meaning they are not capable of reasoning over entities unseen during training. In the real world, there is a resource scarcity, and we cannot train a model with all the nodes of a large KG. Hence, we propose InductWave, a wavelet-based inductive embedding method for logical query answering on large KGs. Here, the training graph consists of fewer nodes than the test graph. Our model performs on par with the baseline models while having half the number of message-passing layers. It outperforms all of them in most cases, with 75% of the layers. These fewer resource requirements enable us to evaluate InductWave on massive graphs, such as Wiki-KG. We test our model using extensive experiments across varying train-test graph proportions of the FB15k-(237) dataset, comparing it with the state-of-the-art models. The code and datasets for the model are available at https://github.com/kracr/inductwave/.

</details>


### [174] [The Blind Curator: How a Biased Judge Silently Disables Skill Retirement in Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07436)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在无参考任务中，当评判模型存在偏差时，自进化代理的技能淘汰机制如何失效。研究表明，虚假通过偏差会关闭基于贡献的技能淘汰，且这种机制失效是普遍存在的，但其对评估质量的影响取决于具体场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自进化代理中，技能淘汰机制依赖于无偏奖励信号来维持性能基线，但在无参考任务中使用的LLM评判往往存在偏差，这可能导致机制失效，因此需要分析其影响。

Method: 通过构建腐败奖励分析框架，并在一个无参考报告写作测试平台上注入特定偏差（如虚假通过），结合代码生成交叉验证进行因果分析和行为研究。

Result: 发现对称噪声不影响技能淘汰，但虚假通过偏差会在超过某一阈值后彻底禁用贡献型淘汰机制；该机制失效具有普适性，仅 verifier-like 评分器可幸免；而对评估质量的影响则依赖于是否同时阻碍技能生成。

Conclusion: 虚假通过偏差会静默地关闭技能淘汰机制，导致潜在安全风险，但不会在总体指标中显现；提出一种低成本的缺陷注入审计方法，可在部署前判断评判模型是否处于危险区域。

Abstract: A self-evolving agent retires its bad skills by watching them fail, so what happens when the judge cannot see the failures? Skill retirement is the structural constraint that keeps a growing library from drifting below the no-skill baseline, but its guarantee assumes an unbiased reward, which is false for the LLM judges that reference-free tasks force upon us. We show that a biased judge does not merely add noise; it \emph{silently switches off the curator}. We make this precise with a corrupted-reward analysis and, isolating the causal channel by injecting corruption on top of a deterministic reward, a behavioral study on a reference-free report-writing testbed with a code-generation cross-check. Symmetric noise leaves retirement intact, but \emph{false-pass} bias (failures slipping through as passes) disables contribution-based retirement past a sharp threshold that no amount of data can cross. Separating genuine retirement from cap-eviction churn shows this \emph{mechanism} failure is universal, holding across domains and failure rates and sparing only near-zero-false-pass, verifier-like graders. The downstream \emph{outcome}, though, is regime-dependent: eval quality degrades only where the same corruption also starves skill synthesis, and otherwise holds steady, so the disabled curator is \emph{silent}, surfacing in no aggregate metric. The contribution is a behavioral safety result, not a performance one. A cheap defect-injection audit then tells an operator, before deployment, which side of the threshold their judge occupies.

</details>


### [175] [SpaCellAgent: A Self-Evolving LLM-Based Multi-Agent Framework for Trajectory Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07467)
*Songhan Wang,Haoang Chi,He Li,Zhiheng Zhang,Jiayan Yuan,Cheems Wang,Hao Peng,Xinwang Liu,Wenjing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpaCellAgent是一个基于大语言模型的多智能体框架，能够自动化完成空间和单细胞转录组数据的轨迹推断分析，显著提升分析效率并降低技术门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹推断方法依赖大量人工干预和多种异构工具的操作经验，限制了高效分析的普及。

Method: 提出SpaCellAgent，采用多智能体架构进行策略性工作流规划，结合动态工具编排引擎自适应选择算法，并通过自我进化模块迭代优化性能。

Result: 在六个异质数据集上验证，SpaCellAgent分析效率提升超过40%，且结果与专家水平一致。

Conclusion: SpaCellAgent实现了从自然语言指令到优化分析流程的全自动转化，推动了计算生物学中可扩展、智能体驱动的新范式。

Abstract: Spatial and Single-cell transcriptomics are transformative in deciphering cellular dynamics. As the fundamental paradigm for reconstructing cell developmental paths, trajectory inference (TI) is critical. However, existing methods require extensive manual intervention and proficiency in heterogeneous tools, posing a significant barrier to efficient TI analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose SpaCellAgent, an autonomous large language model (LLM) multi-agent framework that automates end-to-end spatiotemporal analysis and narrative generation. SpaCellAgent utilizes a multi-agent architecture for strategic workflow planning, a dynamic tool-orchestration engine for adaptive algorithm selection, and a self-evolution module that iteratively refines performance through feedback. We evaluate SpaCellAgent on six heterogeneous datasets encompassing complex temporal developmental trajectories, diverse sequencing platforms, and spatially-resolved tissue architectures. SpaCellAgent consistently demonstrates over 40\% improvement in analytical efficiency while maintaining expert-aligned performance. By converting natural language specifications into optimized analytical workflows and fully automating the pipeline, SpaCellAgent democratizes advanced spatiotemporal modeling and establishes a scalable, agent-driven paradigm for computational biology. The code and materials are available at https://github.com/LittleXH-shw/SpaCellAgent.

</details>


### [176] [Search, Fail, Recover: A Training Framework for Correction-Aware Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07492)
*Dmitry Beresnev,Vladimir Makharev,Roman Khalikov,Ivan Oseledets,Petr Anokhin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pyligent是一个受Diligent Learner启发的训练与推理框架，通过验证搜索部分解链来建模推理过程，利用任务验证器标记生成的延续和失败，并将搜索树转化为监督目标，支持继续、完成和回溯三种动作，显著提升了在存在延迟失败场景下的求解成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多推理任务无法通过单一的从左到右链式思维来充分描述，模型需要能够探索可能分支、观察延迟失败并回溯到可修复的前缀。现有方法主要依赖对完整正确路径的模仿学习，缺乏对失败路径的有效利用。

Method: 提出Pyligent框架，引入任务验证器对生成的部分解进行标注，构建搜索树，并将其转换为包含'继续'、'完成'和'回溯'三种动作的监督信号，同时可选择性地保留废弃分支的推理轨迹作为总结信息。

Result: 在隐藏有向图、4×4数独（含或不含推理轨迹）和Blocksworld等具有精确验证器的任务上，相比仅使用黄金样本的监督微调，Pyligent在求解率上分别提升了72.7、17、18、27、14和13个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式地利用失败分支的监督信号可以有效教会模型恢复行为，超越传统仅模仿完美解链的方法，尤其在需要处理延迟失败的复杂推理任务中表现突出。

Abstract: Many reasoning tasks are not well described by a single left-to-right chain: a solver may need to pursue a plausible branch, observe delayed failure, and return to the latest prefix that can still be completed. We introduce Pyligent, a training and inference framework inspired by the Diligent Learner formulation that represents reasoning as validated search over partial solution chains. A task validator labels generated continuations and failures, and the resulting search trees are converted into supervised targets for three actions: continue, finish, and backtrack, with optional traces that summarize abandoned branches. We evaluate Pyligent on a hidden directed graph task designed to isolate delayed-failure recovery, and on structured reasoning domains with exact validators, including $4{\times}4$ Sudoku, Sudoku with reasoning traces, and Blocksworld. Compared with gold-only supervised fine-tuning, Pyligent improves solve rate by $72.7$ percentage points on hidden graphs, by $17$ and $18$ points on mixed and expert Sudoku, by $27$ and $14$ points on mixed and expert Sudoku with reasoning traces, and by $13$ points on Blocksworld. These results suggest that explicit failed-branch supervision can teach useful recovery behavior beyond imitation of polished solution chains.

</details>


### [177] [Do LLM-Generated Skills Make Better AI Data Scientists? A Component Ablation Across Data-Science Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07504)
*Wei-Jung Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了使用大语言模型（LLM）生成的技能文件是否能提升数据科学任务中的表现，发现在四个生命周期阶段中，LLM生成的技能并未显著优于仅使用任务提示的方法，且各种消融变体也无明显改进，结果表明不应将单次生成的LLM技能作为默认策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索LLM自动生成的技能文件能否作为低维护成本的替代方案，以避免为每个数据科学任务手动编写和维护技能文件所带来的瓶颈。

Method: 在数据准备、提取、统计分析和报告四个阶段各使用一个LLM生成的技能进行测试，并通过消融实验分析不同技能组件的作用；共覆盖56个任务、九种模型配置和三个提供商，进行了7,560次运行，另加1,512次对照实验。

Result: LLM生成的完整技能或其任何消融版本均未显著提升性能（所有p值≥0.396，变体间总差异仅1.2个百分点），且与无关内容的技能格式对照效果相当。

Conclusion: 不建议将单次生成的LLM技能作为数据科学工作流中的默认提示策略，因其未能提供可靠性能增益。

Abstract: Product data scientists often ask LLM-based agents to help with recurring execution tasks such as cleaning data, writing SQL, choosing statistical tests, and formatting results. Reusable skill files are meant to avoid prompting from scratch by packaging guidance for a task family. Expert-written skills can encode high-quality guidance, but writing and maintaining them across many data-science task families creates a manual bottleneck. We ask whether LLM-generated skills offer a useful low-curation alternative: do they improve performance over the task prompt alone? We test this question across four lifecycle stages: data preparation, data extraction, statistical analysis, and reporting, using one generated skill per stage. We find no reliable improvement from full generated skills over No-Skill prompting. We then ask whether any part of the skill is useful by ablating different skill components. The main ablation covers 56 tasks, nine model configurations, and three providers, yielding 7,560 runs. Compared with prompting using the task alone, neither the full generated skill nor any ablated skill variant significantly improves performance; all p-values are at least 0.396, and the total spread across variants is only 1.2 pp. A supplemental token-matched control adds 1,512 runs and finds that Full skills perform similarly to task-irrelevant skill-formatted content. The results caution against using one LLM-generated skill per data-science workflow as a default single-shot prompting strategy.

</details>


### [178] [RL Post-Training Builds Compositional Reasoning Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07646)
*Azwar Abdulsalam,Nishil Patel,Andrew Saxe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 强化学习（RL）后训练不仅能放大基础模型中的原始技能，还能通过分阶段的组合机制将这些原始技能重组为新的、可复用的高级策略，而不仅仅是依赖预训练中的已有能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨强化学习后训练是否仅放大基础模型中已有的原始技能，还是能够将这些技能组合成全新的高层次策略。

Method: 在一个完全可观测的重写语法环境中，对Transformer模型进行原始符号重写链的预训练，并在仅有二值最终答案奖励的情况下进行基于Trace的推理任务的强化学习后训练，同时与拒绝微调等方法进行对比分析。

Result: RL能够在保持问题解决率的同时显著超越预训练模型的表现，发现并巩固顺序和并行的组合程序；Trace分析显示RL通过先加强原始简化、再发现有效组合过程的分阶段机制实现能力跃升；相比产生大量无效捷径的拒绝微调，RL更具选择性地探索有效结构。

Conclusion: RL后训练不仅仅是放大预训练中的原始技能，而是通过有选择性的探索，将弱程序性成分构建成稳定可靠的高级策略，其关键在于预训练是否将原始能力组织为可压缩的简化过程。

Abstract: Does RL post-training merely amplify primitive skills already latent in a base model, or can it compose primitive skills into new higher-level strategies? We study this question in a fully observable rewrite-grammar environment where the pretraining distribution is known and every generated rewrite can be audited. A Transformer is pretrained on primitive symbol-rewrite chains and post-trained on a Trace-based reasoning task with only a binary final-answer reward. RL solves held-out problems that remain rarely solved by the pretrained model even under much larger sampling budgets, while rejection fine-tuning improves early but plateaus. Trace analysis shows that RL reorganizes primitive competence through a phased compositional mechanism: it first strengthens primitive reductions, then discovers valid composed procedures. These include sequential compositions, which collapse ordered chains of primitive contractions, and parallel compositions, which combine independent primitive contractions in a single step. The composed procedures are not isolated samples; they are reused and consolidated into a stable repertoire. Comparing RL with rejection fine-tuning shows that the key difference is not exploration volume but selectivity: RFT produces many shortcut-like rewrites, much of them invalid, whereas RL concentrates exploration into valid reusable structure. Pretraining ablations show that the emergence of compositional strategies is gated not by primitive exposure alone, but by whether pretraining organizes primitive competence into reduction procedures that RL can later compress. The base model provides weak procedural ingredients; RL builds them into reliable higher-level strategies.

</details>


### [179] [Recursive Self-Improvement in AI: From Bounded Self-Refinement to Autonomous Research Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07663)
*Mingguang Chen,Licheng Wang,Bo Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对2024-2026年间1250篇关于AI自我改进的arXiv论文进行了系统性综述，提出一个基于改进对象与闭环程度的二维分类法，区分了有限的自我优化与开放式的递归自我提升（RSI），并强调自评估机制在其中的核心作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于AI自我改进的研究术语混乱，概念混杂，缺乏统一框架来区分不同层次的自我改进目标，亟需清晰的分类体系以指导技术发展与安全治理。

Method: 基于两个维度构建分类体系：一是系统所改进的对象（行为、策略、评估器或研究过程本身），二是闭环程度（从有人类参与到完全自主）。同时建立评估信号的验证层级，分析各类自评估方法的强度与失效模式。

Result: 发现实际的自我改进效果与其评估信号在验证层级中的位置密切相关；低层级的内在自评容易导致自我确认循环、模型崩溃等问题；目前所有所谓的‘自我进化’仍受限于计算、稳定性及人类设定的研究方向等外部锚点。

Conclusion: 真正的递归自我改进尚未实现，当前进展集中在有界自我优化；未来关键挑战在于建立可治理的测量标准，特别是对自我改进能力的可审计、可监管的评估框架。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly participate in their own improvement: revising their outputs, adapting their own harnesses during deployment, training on data they generate, and, increasingly, conducting AI research itself. This literature is described under a vocabulary ("self-refine," "self-reward," "self-play," "self-evolve") that conflates fundamentally different ambitions. We survey 1,250 arXiv papers (2024-2026) along two axes: what the system improves -- its behavior in deployment, its policy through training, its evaluator, or the research process itself -- and the degree of loop closure (human-in-the-loop to fully closed). The taxonomy separates bounded self-refinement -- convergent, evaluable, and already industrial practice -- from open-ended recursive self-improvement (RSI), which remains bounded by grounding requirements, collapse dynamics, and compute constraints on every measured axis.
